Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a cohomologies algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 100. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 747/1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on cohomologies structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 100. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 747. QED.

Theorem 218.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on cohomologies structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 125. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 14 with relations in degree 5.
